<domain>
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?u - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?u)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?u)
      (collect ?z ?u)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<domain>
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj ?w - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj ?w - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

------------------------------
Here is the human-readable version of the plan, following the given action sequence exactly.

1. **At time 1, perform `wretched object_3 object_5 object_6 object_1`.**  
   Use **object_3** to switch its `next` link from **object_5** to **object_6**, relying on the facts that **object_3** is `sneeze`, **object_5** and **object_6** are `texture`, **object_1** is `stupendous`, and both **object_5** and **object_6** are collected by **object_1**.

2. **At time 2, perform `clip object_9 object_3 object_6`.**  
   Since **object_9** is a `hand`, **object_3** is `sneeze`, **object_6** is `texture`, and both **object_3** and **object_9** currently point to **object_6**, make **object_9** form a `vase` relation with **object_3** and remove the link `next object_9 object_6`.

3. **At time 3, perform `wretched object_3 object_6 object_5 object_1`.**  
   Use **object_3** again to switch its `next` link back from **object_6** to **object_5**, using **object_1** as the shared `stupendous` collector for **object_6** and **object_5**.

4. **At time 4, perform `tightfisted object_9 object_3 object_5`.**  
   With **object_9** as `hand`, **object_3** as `sneeze`, **object_5** as `texture`, **object_3** pointing to **object_5**, and `vase object_9 object_3` in place, make **object_9** point to **object_5** and remove the `vase` relation between **object_9** and **object_3**.

5. **At time 5, perform `sip object_9 object_0 object_5`.**  
   Since **object_9** is `hand`, **object_0** is `cats`, **object_5** is `texture`, and both **object_9** and **object_0** point to **object_5**, create `vase object_9 object_0` and remove `next object_9 object_5`.

6. **At time 6, perform `memory object_0 object_5 object_7`.**  
   Because **object_0** is `cats`, **object_5** and **object_7** are `spring`, and **object_0** currently points to **object_5**, switch **object_0** so that it points to **object_7** instead.

  (:types object)

    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)

    (texture ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

    :precondition (and

      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj ?w - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
